    <script>
    $(document).ready(function () {
        $('.swiper.block-swiper').each(function () {
            var uniqueClass = $(this).attr('class').split(' ').pop();
            
            var swiperBlock = new Swiper(this, {
                slidesPerView: "auto",
                spaceBetween: 24,
                breakpoints: {
                    991: {
                        spaceBetween: 12,
                    },
                }
            });

            function updateNavigationButtons() {
                if (swiperBlock.isBeginning) {
                    $('.swiper-button-prev.' + uniqueClass).addClass('disabled');
                } else {
                    $('.swiper-button-prev.' + uniqueClass).removeClass('disabled');
                }
                if (swiperBlock.isEnd) {
                    $('.swiper-button-next.' + uniqueClass).addClass('disabled');
                } else {
                    $('.swiper-button-next.' + uniqueClass).removeClass('disabled');
                }
            }

            updateNavigationButtons();
            $(window).on('resize', updateNavigationButtons);
            swiperBlock.on('transitionEnd', updateNavigationButtons);
            
            $('.swiper-button-next.' + uniqueClass).on('click', function() {
                if (!$(this).hasClass('disabled')) {
                    swiperBlock.slideNext();
                }
            });

            $('.swiper-button-prev.' + uniqueClass).on('click', function() {
                if (!$(this).hasClass('disabled')) {
                    swiperBlock.slidePrev();
                }
            });

        })
        
    });
    </script>
</section><section class="block b-radar darklight " data-mrf-recirculation="Radar dark light">
	<div class="container">
    	    	<h2 class="block-title ">

      function abril_scripts_add() {
        if (abril_scripts_dinamicos_carregados === true) {
          return;
        }
        
        // Usa requestIdleCallback para carregar scripts quando o navegador estiver ocioso
        if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
          requestIdleCallback(() => {
            loadScriptsDynamically();
          }, { timeout: 2000 });
        } else {
          // Fallback para navegadores que não suportam requestIdleCallback
          setTimeout(loadScriptsDynamically, 100);
        }
      }

      function loadScriptsDynamically() {
                  const script_0 = document.createElement('script');

            function abril_scripts_add_click(clallback, param) {
        if (abril_scripts_dinamicos_carregados !== true) {
          abril_scripts_add();
          abril_scripts_dinamicos_carregados = true;
        }
        pcallback = eval("(" + clallback + ")");
        return pcallback(param);
      }

    <script>
    $("span.share-icon").click(function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        let shareTitle = $(this).data("title");
        let shareText = $(this).data("text");
        let shareUrl = $(this).data("url");

        if (navigator.share) {
            navigator
                .share({
                    title: shareTitle,
                    text: shareText,
                    url: shareUrl,
                })
                .then(() => console.log("Share success!"))
                .catch((error) => console.log("Share error!", error));
        } else {
            console.log("Share not supported!");
        }
    });

    	<script>
		jQuery(function($){
			$('.leia-mais-bloco-home').click(function(){

				let nome_bloco = $(this).find('.botao span').text().trim();

				if( $(this).parents('section').hasClass('quatro-colunas') ){

					if( $(this).parent('.col-s-12').hasClass('nao-colapsado') ){
						gaSendEvent({
							"category": "Blocos",
							"action": "Clique para colapsar",
							"label": nome_bloco
						});
					}
					else{
						gaSendEvent({
							"category": "Blocos",
							"action": "Clique para expandir",
							"label": nome_bloco
						});
					}

					$(this).parent('.col-s-12').toggleClass('nao-colapsado');
				}
				else{
					if( $(this).parents('section').hasClass('nao-colapsado') ){
						gaSendEvent({
							"category": "Blocos",
							"action": "Clique para colapsar",
							"label": nome_bloco
						});
					}
					else{
						gaSendEvent({
							"category": "Blocos",
							"action": "Clique para expandir",
							"label": nome_bloco
						});
					}

					$(this).parents('section').toggleClass('nao-colapsado');
				}

				if( $(this).hasClass('leia-mais-unico')){
					if( $(this).find('em').text() === 'Mais em' ) $(this).find('em').text('Menos em');
					else $(this).find('em').text('Mais em');
				}

				blocos_home_veja();
			});

			var blocos_home_veja = function(){

				var blocos = [];

				$('main section.block').each(function(){

							var bloco = [id, 'nao-colapsado'];
						}
						else{
							var bloco = [id, 'colapsado'];
						}

						blocos.push(bloco);
					}
				});

				localStorage.setItem('blocos_colapsados_home_veja', JSON.stringify(blocos));
			};

		var AbrilCustomDimension = (function() {

			function sendCustomDimension(dimension) {

				if (typeof dimension == 'undefined') {

			function tipoAssinante(){
				let tipo = "nao logado";
				if( AbrilCustomDimension.verifyCookie(AbrilCustomDimension.sessionPrefix()) !== '' ){
					tipo = "cadastrado";
				    const prodUser = localStorage.getItem('AbrilProductsUser');
					if(prodUser !== null && prodUser.length > 0){
						tipo = "assinante";
					}
				}
				return tipo;
			}

			function tipoAssinatura(){
				let tipo = "sem assinatura";
				let prodUser = localStorage.getItem('AbrilProductsUser');
				let combo = logged_paywall_combo[0].split(",");
				let digital = logged_paywall[0].split(",");

				if( prodUser !== null ){
					if(prodUser.length > 0){
						direito = prodUser.split(",");

						for(let i = 0; i < digital.length; i++){
							if (direito.indexOf(digital[i].toString()) != -1 && digital[i] != "") {
								tipo = "digital";
							}
						}

						for(let i = 0; i < combo.length; i++){
							if (direito.indexOf(combo[i].toString()) != -1 && combo[i] != "") {
								tipo = "combo";
							}
						}
					}
				}

				return tipo;
			}

			function tipoLogin(){

			function userID(){

			function verifyCookie(c_name, cpar) {
				var name = c_name + "=";
				var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
				for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
					var c = ca[i];
					while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
						c = c.substring(1);
					}
					if (c.indexOf(name) == 0 && cpar == undefined) {
						return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
					}
					if (c.indexOf(name) == 0 && cpar) {
						var arr = [];
						str = c.substring(name.length, c.length);
						str = str.split(":");
						for (var j = 0; j < str.length; j++) {
							a = str[j].split("=");
							arr[a[0]] = a[1];
						}

						if (arr[cpar]) {
							return arr[cpar];
						}
					}
				}
				return "";
			}

			function sendNavigationData() {

				console.log("sendMetricStatus: " + sendMetricStatus);
				console.log("limite_leitura: " + limite_leitura);

				if (!sendMetricStatus && limite_leitura) {
					console.log("caiu no limite!!!!!!!!");
					return sendNavigationDataBq();
				}
				console.log("Espera min_leitura");
				return setTimeout(sendNavigationData, 5000);
			}

			function getCookieIdGlobal(c_name) {
			    if (document.cookie.length > 0) {
			        c_start = document.cookie.indexOf(c_name + "=");
			        if (c_start != -1) {
			            c_start = c_start + c_name.length + 1;
			            c_end = document.cookie.indexOf(";", c_start);
			            if (c_end == -1) {
			                c_end = document.cookie.length;
			            }
			            return unescape(document.cookie.substring(c_start, c_end));
			        }
			    }
			    return "";
			}

			function getLocalStorage(c_name){
				return localStorage.getItem(c_name);
			}

			function setCookieGlobalId(c_name,value){
				var y = new Date();
				y.setTime(y.getTime() + (365*24*60*60*1000));
				expires = "expires="+ y.toUTCString();
				document.cookie = c_name+"="+value+";" + expires + ";domain=.abril.com.br;path=/";
				console.log("Gerou Cokie: " + value);
			}

			function setLocalStorage(c_name,value){
				localStorage.setItem(c_name, value);
				console.log("Gerou LocalStorage: " + value);
			}

			function persistIdGlobal() {

				if ( verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal('abrilUserId') == true ){
					return;
				}

				if( typeof gaGlobal != 'undefined'){
					clientId = gaGlobal.vid;
						console.log("Gerou ID pelo gaGlobal: "+clientId);
						var hoje = new Date().toDateString();
						var value = `${clientId}|${hoje}`;
						setCookieGlobalId('abrilUserId',value);
						setLocalStorage('abrilUserId',value);
						return;
				}

				if (typeof gtag !== 'undefined'){
					gtag('get', 'G-0HTYQ9ZBSJ', 'client_id', (clientId)=> {
						console.log("Gerou ID pelo GA4: "+clientId);
						var hoje = new Date().toDateString();
						var value = `${clientId}|${hoje}`;
						setCookieGlobalId('abrilUserId',value);
						setLocalStorage('abrilUserId',value);
						//localStorage.setItem('abrilUserId', `${clientId}|${hoje}`);

					})
					return;
				}

				setTimeout(function(e) {
					AbrilCustomDimension.persistIdGlobal();
				}, 1000);
			}

			function parseIdGLobalValue(value){
				if(idData = value.split("|")){
					return idData;
				}
				return null;
			}

			function verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal(c_name) {

				var idDataCokie = getCookieIdGlobal(c_name);
				var idDataStorage = getLocalStorage(c_name);

				if( idDataCokie && idDataStorage ){
					if( idDataCokie !== idDataStorage ){
						console.log("IdGlobal: checar ID mais antigo");
						let dataCokie = parseIdGLobalValue(idDataCokie);
						let dataStorage = parseIdGLobalValue(idDataStorage);
						console.log( `${idDataCokie}  ${idDataStorage}` );
						console.log( new Date(dataCokie[1]) < new Date(dataStorage[1]) );
						if( new Date(dataCokie[1]) < new Date(dataStorage[1]) ){
							setLocalStorage(c_name,idDataCokie);
							console.log('gravou valor do cokie no storage' + idDataCokie);
							return true;
						}else{
							setCookieGlobalId(c_name,idDataStorage);
							console.log('gravou valos do storage no cokie ' + idDataStorage);
							return true;
						}
					}
					console.log("IdGlobal: IDs iguais");
					return true;
				}

				if( idDataCokie && !idDataStorage ){
					console.log("IdGlobal: Apenas id cokie");
					setLocalStorage(c_name,idDataCokie);
					return true;
				}

				if( !idDataCokie && idDataStorage ){
					console.log("IdGlobal: Apenas id storage");
					setCookieGlobalId(c_name,idDataStorage);
					return true;
				}

				return false;
				//new Date(new Date().setDate(new Date().getDate() - 5)).toDateString();

			}

			function getUnicIdGlobal() {
				//let idCokie = getCookieIdGlobal('abrilUserId');
				//verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal('abrilUserId');
				let id = null;
				if ( verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal('abrilUserId') == true ){
					let idStorage = localStorage.getItem('abrilUserId');
					let dataId = null;
					if ( idStorage ) {
						if(idData = idStorage.split("|")){
							id = idData[0];
							dataId = idData[1]
						}
						console.log("getUnicIdGlobal: " + id);
						return id;
					}
				}

				// possivel forçar gerar id;
				console.log("getUnicIdGlobal: " + id);
				return false;
			}

			function checkUnicIdGlobal() {
				if (getUnicIdGlobal()) {
					console.log("checkUnicIdGlobal: " + getUnicIdGlobal());
					return true;
				}
				console.log("checkUnicIdGlobal: " + getUnicIdGlobal());
				return false;
			}

			async function sendNavigationDataBq(assinou = false, postAssineId = false, userId = '', bloqueado = false) {

				
								
				if (sendMetricStatus) {
					return;
				}

				var fim_leitura = performance.now();
				var tempo_leitura = (fim_leitura - inicio_leitura) / 1000;
				sendMetricStatus = true;

												
																																								

								
				
				let post_id = '3473475';
				let marca = 'veja';

				assinou = false;

					autenticado = true;

					userId = AbrilCustomDimension.getUnicIdGlobal();

					AbrilCustomDimension.persistIdGlobal();

					bloqueado = AbrilPaywall.ShowPaywall;

				console.log("post id: " + post_id);
				console.log("marca: " + marca);

				if (userId == null || userId == '' || typeof userId == 'undefined' || post_id == null || post_id == '' || typeof post_id == 'undefined' || marca == null || marca == '' || typeof marca == 'undefined') {
					console.log("Dados CW incompletos");
					return;
				}

																												
				let userStorage = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('abrilAssetsUser'));
				if(typeof userStorage === "object" && userStorage !== null){

				if( pathComponentsSlug ){

						pathComponentsSlug.pop();

					'utm_medium': '',
					'utm_campaign': '',
					'crm_id': AbrilCustomDimension.crm_id,
					'utm_crm_id': '',
					'tipo' : customData['tipo-pagina'],
					'slug' : slug,
					'referrer' : referrer_value,
					'source' : ''
				};

				console.log("DATA NOVOS PARANS");
				console.log(data);

				fetch('https://us-central1-abril-id-testes-1528223718961.cloudfunctions.net/navigationData/dados-editoriais', {
						method: "POST",
						body: JSON.stringify(data),
						headers: {
							"Content-type": "application/json; charset=UTF-8"
						}
					})
					.then(response => response.json())
					.then(json => console.log(json))
					.catch(err => console.log(err));
			}

			function getQueryVariable(variable) {
				var query = window.location.search.substring(1);
				var vars = query.split("&");
				for (var i=0;i<vars.length;i++) {
					var pair = vars[i].split("=");
					if(pair[0] == variable){return pair[1];}
			    }
			    return(false);
			}

			function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
				var d = new Date();
				d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
				var expires = "expires=" + d.toUTCString();
				document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + "; path=/;" + expires + "; domain=.abril.com.br";
				console.log("Gerou Cookie: " + cname);
				return;
			}
			
			function sessionPrefix(){
				let prefix_amb = "";
	        	if(typeof assine_environment[0] !== 'undefined'){
	        		if(assine_environment[0] !== 'sandbox'){
	        			prefix_amb = "homolog_";
	        		}
	        	}
	        	return prefix_amb + 'session';
	        }

			return {
				sendCustomDimension: sendCustomDimension,
				sendNavigationData: sendNavigationData,
				verifyCookie: verifyCookie,
				setCookie: setCookie,
				getQueryVariable: getQueryVariable,
				persistIdGlobal: persistIdGlobal,
				checkUnicIdGlobal: checkUnicIdGlobal,
				getUnicIdGlobal: getUnicIdGlobal,
				crm_id: crm_id,
				tipoAssinante: tipoAssinante,
				tipoAssinatura: tipoAssinatura,
				tipoLogin: tipoLogin,
				userID: userID,
				verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal: verifyCrossDomainIdGlobal,
				sessionPrefix: sessionPrefix
			};
		})();

		if ( !AbrilCustomDimension.checkUnicIdGlobal()) {
			AbrilCustomDimension.persistIdGlobal();
		}

	</script>

function scripts_add_click(clallback, param){
    if( scripts_dinamicos_carregados !== true){
        scripts_add();
        scripts_dinamicos_carregados = true;
    }
    if( typeof AbrilLogin === 'undefined' ){
        return setTimeout(() => {
         scripts_add_click(clallback, param);
        }, "1000");
    }
    pcallback = eval("(" + clallback + ")");
    return pcallback(param);
}

$(".user-alert-close img").on("click", function () {
  $('#user-exp-ctrl').css("pointerEvents", "auto")
  $("#header-user-exp-ctrl").css("pointerEvents", "auto");
  $("#user-exp-menu").removeClass("show");
  $("#user-exp-menu-fade").removeClass("show");
  $("html").toggleClass("user-exp-overflow");
  $("#user-exp-login-spinner").toggleClass("show");
  if($(".user-alert-icon img").length > 0){
    $(".user-alert-icon img").css("display", "none");
  }
  if($(".user-alert-icon").length > 0){
    $(".user-alert-icon").css("display", "none");
  }
});

var AbrilNotificacoes = (function () {
    var cokieName = "notificationsControl";
    var storageName = "abrilNotificacoes";
    function init(){
        const data_info = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('abrilNotificacoes'));
        console.log('AbrilNotificacoes.init()');

        if(data_info===null){
            $('#user-exp-ctrl .user-avatar').css('display', 'none');
            $('.user-avatar-name').css('display','block')
            $('#user-exp-ctrl .user-avatar').css('display','none');
            $('.user-avatar.user-avatar-notification').css('display', 'none');
            $("#user-exp-alerts-wrapper").css('visibility','hidden');
            $(".user-exp-detail").css('border-radius','0 0 4px 4px');
            return;
        }

        $("#user-exp-login-spinner").removeClass("loadExpLoggedSpinner");
        if (data_info.avatar) {
            $('.user-avatar-name').css('display','none');
            $('#user-exp-ctrl .user-avatar').attr('src', data_info.avatar).css('display', 'block');
            $('.user-avatar.user-avatar-notification').attr('src', data_info.avatar).css('display', 'block');
            $(".user-alert-icon").addClass("user-alert-foto");
        } else {
            $('.user-avatar-name').css('display','block')
            $('#user-exp-ctrl .user-avatar').css('display','none');
            $('.user-avatar.user-avatar-notification').css('display', 'none');
            $(".user-alert-icon").removeClass("user-alert-foto");
        }

        if(typeof data_info.visualizado !=="undefined"){
            if(data_info.visualizado === true){
                $("#user-exp-ctrl").attr("data-visualizado", true);
                $(".user-alert-icon").css("display", "none");
            }else{
                $("#user-exp-ctrl").attr("data-visualizado", false);
                $(".user-alert-icon").css("display", "inline-block");
            }
        }

        let perfilUrl = 'https://minha.abril.com.br';
        if(assine_environment[0]==='sandbox'){
            perfilUrl = 'https://homolog-minha.abril.com.br';
        }

        $('.user-exp-account a').attr('href', perfilUrl);

        if(typeof data_info.notifications === 'undefined'){
            $("#user-exp-alerts-wrapper").css('visibility','hidden');
            $(".user-exp-detail").css('border-radius','0 0 4px 4px');
            return;
        }

        if(data_info.notifications === null){
            $("#user-exp-alerts-wrapper").css({'visibility':'hidden'});
            $(".user-exp-detail").css({'border-radius':'0 0 4px 4px'});
            return;
        }

        return AbrilNotificacoes.montar(data_info.notifications, perfilUrl)
    }

    function montar(notifications, perfilUrl){
        let htmlMsg = '';
        let temCritico = '';
        if(notifications.length >= 1){
            $("#user-exp-alerts-wrapper").css("visibility", "visible");
            $("#user-exp-notification-red").css("visibility", "visible");
            let total = notifications.length;

            const unicNotifications = Object.values(
            notifications.reduce((acc, obj) => ({ ...acc, [obj.dscTituloNotificacao]: obj }), {})
            );
            
            unicNotifications.forEach((msg, i) => {
                console.log(msg);
                let titulo = msg.dscTituloNotificacao? msg.dscTituloNotificacao : msg.codTipoNotificacao;
                let critico = msg.critico;
                if ('sim' === critico) {
                    temCritico = 'sim';
                }

                let bg = msg.codCorTextoNotificacao? ' style="background-color: '+msg.codCorTextoNotificacao+'"': '';
                if(i<=1){
                    if ('sim' === critico) {
                        htmlMsg += '<div class="user-exp-row user-alert-critico">';
                    }
                    else {
                        htmlMsg += '<div class="user-exp-row">';
                    }
                        htmlMsg += '<div class="info">';
                            htmlMsg += '<p class="user-alert-title"><mark'+bg+'>'+titulo+'</mark></p>';
                        htmlMsg += '</div>';
                        if(msg.txtNotificacao != null){
                            htmlMsg += '<div class="textlink"><a href="'+msg.dscUrlMinhaAbril+'">';
                            htmlMsg += convertHtml(msg.txtNotificacao) + '</a></div>';
                        }
                    htmlMsg += '</div>';
                }
                if(i==2){
                    htmlMsg += '<div class="user-exp-row"><div class="info-more">';
                    htmlMsg += '<p class="user-alert-title">Você tem <span class="user-alert-qt">+ alertas</span>. ';
                    htmlMsg += '<a href="'+perfilUrl+'/mural/" target="blank">Saiba mais...</a></p></div></div>';
                }
            });
        }
        $("#user-exp-alerts-wrapper").html('<div class="user-exp-menu-fade-alerts"></div>' +htmlMsg);
        return;
    }

    function criar(userID, mostrar=false) {
        console.log("AbrilNotificacoes.cria notificações storage", mostrar);
        if (Number.isInteger(userID) !== true) {
            console.log("AbrilNotificacoes.cria userID invalido");
            return;
        }
        
        sendAjax(userID, false);
        if(mostrar === true){
            AbrilNotificacoes.init();
        }
    }

    async function sendAjax(userID, visualizar=false){
        console.log("AbrilNotificacoes: sendAjax");
        if( checkCokieNotifications() && checkStorageNotifications() ){
            console.log("AbrilNotificacoes: ja existe storage não criar");
            init();
            return;
        }
        
        const myHeaders = new Headers();
        myHeaders.append("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
        
        const data = new URLSearchParams();
        data.append("action", "get_notification_user");
        data.append("_token", document.getElementById("notification_user_ajax_nonce").value);
        data.append("crm", userID);
        
        const requestOptions = {
            method: "POST",
            headers: myHeaders,
            body: data
        }
        
        const response = await fetch(ajaxurl, requestOptions)
        .then((response) => response.json())
        .then((result) => {
            console.log(result)
            salvar(result);
            createCokieNotifications();
            console.log("AbrilNotificacoes: notificações criadas");
        })
        .catch((error) => {
            console.error(error)
            console.log("AbrilNotificacoes: Erro Notifications 1");
        });
        init();
    }

    function ler(userID){
        console.log("AbrilNotificacoes.ler notificações storage");
        if (Number.isInteger(userID) !== true) {
            console.log("AbrilNotificacoes.ler userID invalido");
            return;
        }

        $("#user-exp-ctrl").attr("data-visualizado", true);
        $(".user-alert-icon").css("display", "none");

        console.log("AbrilNotificacoes: sava 3");
        changeStorageStatusLida();
        //sendAjax(userID, true);
    }

    function salvar(data){
        if (!data) {
            return;
        }
        localStorage.setItem("abrilNotificacoes", JSON.stringify(data));
    }

    function excluir(){
        localStorage.removeItem('abrilNotificacoes');
        deleteCokieNotifications();
        $("#user-exp-ctrl").attr("data-visualizado", true);
        $(".user-alert-icon").css("display", "none");
        console.log('AbrilNotificacoes.excluir');
        return init();
    }

    function convertHtml(text){
        text = text.replaceAll(/&lt;/g,'<');
        text = text.replaceAll(/&gt;/g,'>');
        text = text.replaceAll(/&amp;/g,'&');
        text = text.replaceAll(/&quot;/g,'"');
        return text;
    }

    function createCokieNotifications(){
        var d = '';
        var value = true;
        d = new Date();
        d.setTime( d.getTime() + (1000*60*60*24)) ;
        expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();
        return document.cookie = cokieName+"="+value+";" + expires + ";domain=.abril.com.br;path=/";
    } 

    function checkCokieNotifications(){
          var nameEQ = cokieName + "=";
          var ca = document.cookie.split(";");
          for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == " ") c = c.substring(1, c.length);
            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) {
              return true;
            }
          }
       
        return false;
    }
    
    function deleteCokieNotifications() {
        let cookie_params = cokieName +"=" +
           ";domain=.abril.com.br" + 
           "expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:01 GMT";
        document.cookie = cookie_params;
    }

    function checkStorageNotifications(){
        if( localStorage.getItem(storageName) ){
          return true;
        }
        return false;
    }  

    function changeStorageStatusLida(){
        if( data = localStorage.getItem(storageName) ){
            data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageName));
            data.visualizado =true;
            localStorage.setItem(storageName, JSON.stringify(data));
        }
        return ;
    }
    
    function checkNotificationsExists(){
        return checkCokieNotifications() && checkStorageNotifications();
    }
    
    return {
        init: init,
        montar, montar,
        criar: criar,
        excluir: excluir,
        ler: ler,
        changeStorageStatusLida: changeStorageStatusLida,
        check: checkNotificationsExists
    };
})();
</script>

        var AbrilUserHeader = (function() {

            function setMessage(mensagem, name, line, tipo) {
                cor = "#000000";

                    cor = "#FFFFFF";
                }
                console.log("%c" + name + " | linha: " + line + "\n" + mensagem,

            function corTipo(tipo) {
                switch (tipo) {
                    case "alert":
                        cor = '#FF8000'
                        break;
                    case "alert":
                        cor = '#D90000'
                        break;
                    case "success":
                        cor = '#00B200'
                        break;
                    default:
                        cor = "#FFFFFF";
                }
                return cor;
            }

            function montarHeaderLogged(userBundle) {
                const dataUser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageUser));
                console.log("montarHeaderLogged", userBundle);

                            nomeSobrenome = nomPessoaFirstName;
                        }
                        if (qtdNomes > 1) {

                        objHideShow("#user-exp-ctrl .user-avatar-name", "inline");
                        objValue("#userName", "OLÁ, " + nomeSobrenome, "text");
                        objValue(".userNameExp", nomPessoaFirstName, "text");
                        objValue("#userNameExpToggled", dataUser.nome.toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()), "text");
                        objValue("#userExpEmail", dataUser.email, "text");
                    }
                }
                if(document.getElementById("header-user-exp-ctrl") !== null) {
                    objHideShow(".hot-topics-user-exp", "block");
                }
                objAddRemoveClass('body', 'add', 'logged-block');
                objHideShow("#login", "none");
                objHideShow("#assinemenu", "none");
                objHideShow("#logintop", "none");
                objHideShow(".sidebarMenu .assine-btn", "none");
                objHideShow(".assinante", "none");
                objHideShow("#user-exp-login", "none");
                objHideShow("#logout", "inline");
                objHideShow("#logout2", "inline");
                objHideShow("#logout3", "inline");
                objAddRemoveClass('body', 'add', 'logged');
                objHideShow(".search-area a:not(.btn-assine-abril)", "none");
                $('body').addClass('logged');
                if(window.innerWidth < 766){
                    objHideShow(".assinetop", "none");
                }
                if($("#header-user-exp-ctrl").length) {
                    $("#header-user-exp-ctrl").show();
                    $(".assinante").hide();
                }
                
                if(userBundle === true) {
                    if(AbrilNotificacoes.check()){
                        AbrilNotificacoes.init();
                    }else{
                        AbrilNotificacoes.criar(dataUser.crm, true);
                    }
                }
                return;
            }

            function montarHeaderNotLogged() {
                objPointerEvents(".sub-header .search-area a");
                objHideShow("#login", "block");
                objHideShow(".assinetop", "flex");
                objHideShow("#assinemenu", "block");
                objHideShow("#logintop", "block");
                objHideShow("#logout", "block");
                objHideShow(".buscaVivo", "class", "block");
                objHideShow("#logout2", "none");
                objHideShow("#logout3", "none");
                objHideShow("#assine_top", "block");
                objHideShow("#logout", "none");
                objHideShow("#logout", "none");
                objHideShow(".sub-header .search-area a", "block");
                objAddRemoveClass(".sub-header .search-area a", "remove", "hide-s");
                objAddRemoveClass("body", "remove", "logged");
                objValue(".userNameExp", "Usuário", "text");
                objValue("#userName", "Usuário", "text");
                if(window.innerWidth < 766){
                    objHideShow(".assinetop", "none");
                    objAddRemoveClass(".mobile-assine", "add", "on");
                }
                return;
            }

            function objHideShow(name, value) {
                const objs = document.querySelectorAll(name);
                objs.forEach(obj => {
                    obj.style.display = value;
                });
            }

            function objAddRemoveClass(name, action, value) {
                const objs = document.querySelectorAll(name);
                objs.forEach(obj => {
                    if (action === 'add') {
                        obj.classList.add(value);
                    }
                    if (action === 'remove') {
                        obj.classList.remove(value);
                    }
                });
            }

            function objPointerEvents(name) {
                const objs = document.querySelectorAll(name);
                objs.forEach(obj => {
                    obj.style.pointerEvents = 'initial';
                });
            }

            function objValue(name, value, type = 'text') {
                const objs = document.querySelectorAll(name);
                objs.forEach(obj => {
                    if (type === 'text') {
                        obj.innerText = value;
                    }
                    if (type === 'value') {
                        obj.value = value;
                    }
                });
            }

            function getUser(userBundle = false) {
                let userStorage = localStorage.getItem(storageUser);
                let dateNow = new Date().toLocaleString("pt-BR");
                if(userStorage !== null) {
                    userBundle = true;
                }
                if (userBundle === true) {
                    return AbrilUserHeader.montarHeaderLogged(true);
                } else if (userStorage !== null) {
                    return AbrilUserHeader.montarHeaderLogged(false);
                }
                const ofertaDiv = document.querySelector('.menu-oferta-aovivo');
                if (ofertaDiv) {
                    ofertaDiv.style.display = 'block';
                }
                return AbrilUserHeader.montarHeaderNotLogged();
            }

            return {
                setMessage,
                getUser,
                montarHeaderLogged,
                montarHeaderNotLogged,
                objAddRemoveClass
            }
        })();
        AbrilUserHeader.getUser(false);

    <script>
	  function isBlockedUrlPushNews(pageUrl = '') {
		const page = window.location.href;
		let blockedUrls = [
			"abril.com.br/ofertas",
			"abril.com.br/assinar",
			"abril.com.br/login"
		];
		
		if(pageUrl !== '') {
			blockedUrls = [pageUrl];
		}

		for (let i = 0; i < blockedUrls.length; i++) {
			if (page.indexOf(blockedUrls[i]) !== -1) {
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	  }

<script>
(function persistIndexaClickId() {
    // Get the value of a specific query parameter
    const getQueryParam = function(param) {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        return urlParams.get(param);
    };

    // Set a cookie
    const setCookie = function(name, value, days) {
        let expires = "";
        if (days) {
            const date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
            expires = "; expires=" + date.toUTCString();
        }
        document.cookie = name + "=" + (value || "") + expires + "; path=/; domain=.abril.com.br";
    };

    // Get the indexaclickid from the URL
    const indexaClickId = getQueryParam('indexaclickid');

    // Check if indexaclickid has content and set it as a cookie
    if (indexaClickId) {
        setCookie('indexaclickid', indexaClickId, 2); // Stores the cookie for 2 days
    }
})();
